This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [172] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单的两阶段方案"OPD-then-RL"（先用在线策略蒸馏OPD，再用可验证奖励强化学习RLVR），在逻辑和数学推理基准上一致优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有联合优化的基线方法。通过pass@k行为、学习动态和参数更新的系统分析，作者揭示了OPD先扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖，RL再在该支持范围内锐化，而联合优化两个信号会导致相互干扰的机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）和OPD（在线策略蒸馏）是当前推理大模型后训练的两大主流方法。已有工作尝试通过加权相加或教师调制重缩放RL优势等方式，在单一步骤内融合两者的稀疏RL奖励和密集token级监督信号，但效果有限且缺乏机理理解。作者希望探索一种更简洁有效的组合方式，并系统揭示两种信号相互作用的本质。

Method: 提出一个简单两阶段训练方案：先执行OPD训练（利用教师模型的密集token级监督信号），再切换到RLVR训练（利用稀疏的可验证奖励）。同时设计了一系列消融与诊断实验，通过pass@k指标、训练过程中的学习动态跟踪以及参数更新分析，从机理层面解释两阶段优于联合优化的原因。此外还探讨了从OPD切换到RL的最佳时机（以OPD验证分数为准则）以及OPD作为冷启动优于SFT的经验发现。

Result: 实验表明两阶段OPD-then-RL方案在逻辑和数学推理基准上均超越纯OPD、纯RLVR及所有联合基线。机理分析揭示：OPD阶段扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖范围，RL阶段在该支持范围内进行锐化；而联合优化两个信号时会产生干扰。研究还发现OPD验证分数是切换到RL阶段的最佳时机信号，且OPD比SFT是更好的RL冷启动方式。

Conclusion: 作者将原本纠缠的两个优化信号转化为互补的两阶段，确立OPD-then-RL作为一种简单而强大的组合策略，为推理LLM的后训练提供了一种清晰、可解释且实践可行的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [173] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"最后翻译基准"(Last Translation Benchmark, LTB)，一个由人工编写并经同行评审的活数据集，包含能击败当前领先机器翻译模型的文本、图像、音频和视频示例。每个示例附带手工制作的验证规则来精确定位具体失败案例，从而提供可靠且可操作的评估，并支持持续贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译基准趋于饱和，自动评估指标不可靠且易受奖励黑客攻击，甚至人工评估也缺乏可重复性、客观性和可扩展性，阻碍了客观跟踪领域进展和识别改进路径。

Method: 构建一个人类编写、同行评审的多模态示例集，每个示例包含针对该示例具体失败案例的手工验证规则，采用活数据集形式持续接受贡献，并规划版本迭代（当前为LTBv1，截止2026年9月1日前的数据）。

Result: 发布了LTBv1版本，包含截止2026年9月1日前被接受的贡献，未来版本将随新数据持续更新。

Conclusion: LTB提供了一种超越饱和度基准的新评估范式，通过可操作、可靠的案例级验证来追踪机器翻译模型的真实局限与进步。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [174] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验研究大语言模型（LLM）预训练中的知识获取机制，提出辅助视角（auxiliary views，即知识的重述形式）对学习具有因果帮助作用。研究发现：重复是知识获取的必要条件、将token从文档重复分配到辅助视角反而能改善学习效果、辅助视角的有效性不依赖生成它的教师模型强度，并识别了有助于填补知识缺口的上下文性和基础性知识形式，最后从层间偏置和压缩角度解释了其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM在预训练阶段如何获取知识仍存在理解空白，作者希望通过隔离变量来揭示知识获取的关键机制，特别是数据多样性为何重要的深层原因。

Method: 设计受控实验：1）验证重复的必要性并比较重复与释义在不同batch size下的效果；2）在固定token预算下，将token从文档重复转移到辅助视角；3）变化生成辅助视角的教师模型强度；4）识别不同类型的知识（上下文性和基础性）在知识缺口存在时的作用；5）通过层间偏置和压缩分析机制的实现方式。

Result: 确认重复是知识获取的必要条件；释义仅在较小batch size时有利于学习；将token从重复分配到辅助视角可提升学习效果（甚至对事实回忆任务也是如此）；辅助视角有效性不受教师模型强度制约；找到了有助于克服知识缺口的特定知识类型。

Conclusion: 辅助知识表示（自然存在于大规模预训练语料中）是预训练成功的关键因素，这为"数据多样性为何重要"提供了合理解释。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [175] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理链中文本是否真正编码了推理步骤的功能重要性。作者将步骤重要性操作化为"优势"（advantage），即包含某步骤对期望奖励的改变，通过蒙特卡洛回滚估计。研究发现，能力足够强的LLM作为评判者能超越流行率基线，但远未达到噪声上限；微调步骤级批评模型对错误响应有明显改善，但对正确响应仍远低于上限，表明步骤重要性只能从推理文本中部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的研究将思维链推理痕迹视为可解释窗口，用LLM评判者诊断错误、评估忠实性、提供步骤级监督（如过程奖励模型和生成式批评者）。但这些实践隐含假设推理步骤文本携带其功能角色的信息。核心问题是：文本是否真的编码了哪些推理步骤重要的信息？

Method: 将推理步骤的重要性操作化为优势（advantage），即包含该步骤导致的期望奖励变化（如产生正确答案），通过蒙特卡洛回滚估算。以此作为ground truth，评估LLM评判者识别高优势步骤的能力，并比较流行率基线与噪声上限。此外，微调一个步骤级批评模型测试其性能。

Result: 能力足够强的LLM可超越流行率基线但远不及噪声上限；微调步骤级批评模型在错误响应上改善显著，但对正确响应仍远离上限。表明步骤重要性仅部分可从未被验证的推理文本中恢复。

Conclusion: 研究证实推理痕迹的可读性不应被视为可解释性，尤其对过程奖励建模有重要警示意义。步骤重要性仅部分可从文本中恢复，需要谨慎对待基于推理文本的步骤级监督。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESPO（Error-Structured Prompt Optimization），针对进化式提示词优化器（如GEPA）存在的"提示词膨胀"问题，将优化过程分解为"诊断（聚类错误模式）、提议（四种互补策略生成候选）、选择（bootstrap稳定性选择）"三个阶段。在7个NLP基准上平均准确率提升+3.76个百分点（74.67% vs 70.91%），提示词缩短47%，推理更快，且跨模型实验表现一致最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式提示词优化器（如GEPA）存在提示词膨胀问题：每轮迭代不断追加规则和注意事项，导致提示词长度增至原先3倍却未提升准确率。作者追溯出三个根本缺陷：不完整的错误观察、有限的搜索多样性、以及不可靠的选择机制，亟需一种结构化的优化方法来克服这些局限。

Method: ESPO将提示词优化分解为三个明确阶段：(1) Diagnose阶段——在单轮内将所有训练错误聚类为结构化的错误模式；(2) Propose阶段——通过四种具有独立偏置的互补策略生成候选提示词，确保搜索多样性；(3) Select阶段——应用bootstrap稳定性选择机制进行可靠筛选。论文还提供了泛化界，将每个阶段对应到测试时间差距的对应项。

Result: 在7个公共NLP基准（Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer、PUPA）上，ESPO平均准确率较SOTA提高+3.76个百分点（74.67% vs 70.91%），每个数据集均匹配或超越GEPA，同时提示词缩短47%（1004 vs 1878字符）且推理更快。在四个额外学生模型（Gemma 3 12B、Mistral 14B、Qwen3 32B、Claude Haiku 4.5）上均取得最佳平均准确率，最大差距出现在Qwen3 GSM8K（15.00%→91.40%）。消融实验验证了关键预测：仅增加多样性而无bootstrap选择反而损害性能（-1.20%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过结构性诊断错误、多样化生成候选和稳定性选择，有效解决了提示词膨胀问题，在准确率、提示词长度和推理效率上全面超越现有方法。泛化界从理论上将三个阶段与测试时间差距的各组成部分建立了联系，消融实验证实了各组件（尤其是bootstrap选择）的不可或缺性。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"compile by training"方法，将自然语言描述编译为可复用的神经函数。通过在编译时让教师模型生成任务特定示例来训练小型适配器，使生成的函数无需教师模型即可运行，可像普通软件一样存储、版本化和组合。在FuzzyBench-Hard上达到83.6%语义准确率，编译成本约一分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多常见的文本函数容易描述但难以用规则实现，而每次调用大型远程模型会带来重复的成本、延迟和对提供商的依赖。

Method: 编译时，教师模型生成任务特定示例，用于训练针对紧凑解释器的小型适配器。生成的函数运行时不依赖教师模型，可存储、版本化和组合。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard上达到83.6%的语义准确率，优于"Program-as-Weights"快速编译器（无精确匹配）。编译耗时约一分钟而非数秒。

Conclusion: 该方法在准确率和编译成本之间取得权衡，并已在公共交互服务中部署，应用于多站点网站助手、语言控制的3D化身及双向英语-Claudish翻译器等实际场景。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [CAPQ-FAST: Content-Adaptive Perceived Quality Assessment for Faster Audiovisual Playback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03498)
*Jiarun Song,Yuxin Song,Fuzheng Yang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文通过主观实验研究了视频、音频和视听内容在加速播放下的感知质量，提取内容特定的时间动态特征（视频的TI、语音的WPM、音乐的BPM）分别构建预测模型，并提出统一的内容自适应感知质量评估模型CAPQ-FAST，实验验证其能有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线视听服务普遍提供加速播放功能，但不同模态内容（视频、语音、音乐、视听）在加速播放中对可理解性和信息完整性要求不同，导致感知质量存在显著差异。然而，不同播放速度下用户感知质量的研究尚不充分，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 首先开展一系列主观实验以分析播放速度与视频、音频和视听内容感知质量之间的关系；提取内容特定的内在特征来捕捉时间动态，包括视频的时间信息（TI）、语音的每分钟字数（WPM）和音乐的每分钟节拍数（BPM）；分别为视频、语音和音乐开发加速播放下感知质量的预测模型；通过整合这些模型提出统一的内容自适应感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的CAPQ-FAST模型能够有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量。

Conclusion: 该模型可帮助服务提供商更好地理解用户在加速播放下的观看意图和感知体验，从而实现更自适应的个性化推荐和播放控制，提升用户体验的自适应能力。

Abstract: Faster playback has become a common feature in modern online audiovisual services, allowing users to consume content in less time while still maintaining a coherent viewing experience. However, different modalities of media content, such as video, audio (including speech and music), and audiovisual, exhibit varying requirements for understandability and information integrity under faster playback. These differences lead to noticeable variations in perceived quality depending on the content type. Nevertheless, users' perceived quality at different playback speeds remains insufficiently investigated. To address this gap, this paper conducts a series of subjective experiments to analyze the relationship between playback speed and perceived quality for video, audio, and audiovisual content. Content-specific intrinsic features are extracted to capture temporal dynamics, including temporal information (TI) for video, words per minute (WPM) for speech, and beats per minute (BPM) for music. Predictive models of perceived quality under faster playback are then developed separately for video, speech, and music. By integrating these models, a unified content-adaptive perceived quality assessment model (CAPQ-FAST) is proposed for faster audiovisual playback. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model effectively predicts perceived quality under different playback speeds. This model can help service providers better understand users' viewing intentions and perceptual experiences under faster playback, thereby enabling more adaptive personalized recommendations and playback control to enhance user experience adaptability.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析3,553个SWE-chat会话，量化了编码代理中"后期需求到达"（即在实现后用户才提出需求）对代码失效的影响，发现需求到达引发的代码失效约为非需求编辑的两倍，且该负担在会话中无显著下降趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理常在用户完整阐明需求之前就实施代码更改，但现有基准测试在设计上固定需求于实现之前，观测性研究也只报告了用户反馈频率而未将其与代码失效相关联。作者希望将后期需求出现量化为代码失效的可测量来源。

Method: 分析3,553个符合条件的SWE-chat会话，沿三个维度编码实现后到达的需求；在可重放仓库状态的场景中，将每次需求到达与代理先前编写行的删除或替换（作为失效代理指标）关联。辅以受控实验，比较延迟披露与提前警告两种条件下的实现覆盖情况。

Result: 需求到达后引发的失效约为匹配的非需求编辑的两倍，经用户轮次和净删除检查后依然稳健（虽未证明因果关系）。该负担在会话中无显著下降，且考虑多重性后与操作类型无显著关联。受控实验表明延迟披露会将实现转移到揭示之后，而提前警告未检测到覆盖效应。

Conclusion: 后期需求出现被确立为代码失效的可测量来源，为编码代理的需求工程问题提供了量化证据，但也指出部分置信区间较宽、因果关系尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [180] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eiffel-tools，一个针对Eiffel编程语言的语言服务器协议（LSP）实现，它利用大型语言模型（LLMs）辅助开发经过静态验证的软件。该工具提供交互式和非交互式命令来生成代码和规格说明，结合语言和项目特定知识精确引导LLM，并使用静态验证器验证输出。在2个公共数据集上使用3个模型评估了其缺陷修复能力，能修复76%至95%的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的软件验证方法虽然能保证程序正确性，但编写规格说明和修复缺陷非常耗时费力。作者希望利用LLM的强大生成能力，同时通过形式化验证器确保输出的正确性，从而提升开发静态验证软件的效率。

Method: Eiffel-tools作为LSP实现，提供交互式和非交互式命令来生成代码和规格说明。其核心方法包括：利用语言和项目特定知识构造丰富的程序化提示词来精确引导LLM；使用静态验证器验证LLM输出；对未通过验证的输出进行纠正或拒绝；自动处理重试直到程序通过验证为止。

Result: 在2个公共数据集上使用3个模型进行评估，该工具结合LLM和形式化验证器可以修复76%至95%的缺陷，具体效果取决于所使用的模型和提示词。结果还揭示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: LLM与形式化验证器的结合能够有效提升缺陷修复的成功率，Eiffel-tools证明了这种"生成-验证-纠正"循环在静态验证软件开发中的可行性，但实际应用中需要在尝试次数（成本）与成功率之间做出权衡。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [181] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过全因子3x3x3实验设计（共27种组合），系统研究了输出格式、人格设定和紧迫感框架三种提示词约束如何联合影响LLM代码生成的可靠性。研究覆盖5个OpenAI模型（GPT-4o系列、GPT-4.1系列和o3-mini），在164个HumanEval+编码问题上进行22,140次贪心解码评估。结果表明，复合约束可产生架构依赖的退化效应，无法从单因素实验中预测：GPT-4o系列表现出持续的超加性退化（pass@1降低3-12个百分点），GPT-4.1系列则高度抵抗，而o3-mini展现出结构化输出约束反而提升性能的独特模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于软件工程流水线中的代码生成，而实际生产环境的提示词通常同时包含多种约束（如输出格式、人格设定、紧迫感）。现有研究大多只考察单一因素对LLM性能的影响，忽视了多约束组合可能产生的交互效应。作者希望通过全因子实验揭示复合提示词约束是否会产生超出单因素可预测范围的退化，从而填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用受控的3x3x3全因子设计，评估输出格式（JSON/XML/无格式）、人格设定（专家/非专家/无）和紧迫感（高/中/无）三种因素共27种组合。将每种复合条件分解为加性预测和残差交互项，以捕捉超加性退化。使用全部164个HumanEval+问题和5个OpenAI模型（GPT-4o家族、GPT-4.1家族、o3-mini），共22,140次贪心解码评估。通过格式感知的提取流水线区分格式失败与推理失败，并使用McNemar检验、比值比和95%置信区间评估显著性。

Result: 复合约束可产生架构依赖的、无法从单因素实验预测的退化。GPT-4o家族表现出一致的超加性效应，pass@1降低超出加性预测3-12个百分点，最大交互效应为GPT-4o-mini上JSON+专家人格+中等紧迫感的-12.2个百分点。JSON组合通常比XML产生更大的交互效应。GPT-4.1家族对此高度抵抗，而o3-mini呈现定性不同的模式——结构化输出约束反而可提升性能。

Conclusion: LLM对复合提示词约束的脆弱性是架构依赖而非规模依赖的；单独中性或有益的约束组合在一起时可能导致显著退化；复合提示词测试应成为LLM辅助工程流水线可靠性评估的标准实践。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [182] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对现成大语言模型（LLM）在需求质量评估任务上的表现进行了基准测试。基于INCOSE质量标准构建专家级ground truth，评估了OpenAI和Anthropic两个家族的十种模型，跨越一百次独立运行、两个需求集和五个采样温度。结果显示LLM错误剖面高度不对称（最佳模型仅检测到47%的问题，误报11%），在需要系统工程判断的领域表现显著退化，且模型代际进步是非单调的——较新的模型并不一定更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程（RE）质量决定系统工程（SE）下游一切质量，有缺陷的需求会传播为设计返工、进度延误和成本超支。由于需求通常以自然语言书写，生成式AI的进展让人期待LLM能承担需求质量评估这一原本缓慢且高度依赖人类专业知识的任务。然而关于LLM能否被信任承担此任务的实证证据仍然稀缺，这是本研究的直接动机。

Method: 基于INCOSE质量标准构建专家级ground truth，对十个跨两个家族（OpenAI和Anthropic）、五个代际的模型进行基准测试，涉及一百次独立运行、两个需求集和五个采样温度参数。

Result: 四项贡献：(1) 错误剖面高度不对称，最佳Anthropic模型仅中位检测47%问题，误报11%；(2) 在需要SE判断的necessity和correctness问题上几乎总是漏检；(3) 代际进步非单调，新模型未必更好；(4) 错误行为随采样温度变化有限且非单调，表明是模型固有缺陷而非随机性所致。

Conclusion: 现成LLM尚不可作为可信赖的自主评估者。对于Agentic AI开发者而言，将这些LLM模块编排到专门架构中可能加剧而非纠正其缺陷。近期内可辩护的角色是作为人在回路（human-in-the-loop）决策支持工具。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [183] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM代码生成中的"幻觉"现象，将其定义为"无根基生成"（ungrounded generation），与普通代码错误区分。提出了包含根基性、表现层面和行为三个维度的分类体系，并构建了对抗性测试套件（270个不可满足提示+91个可解对照组，6种语言、24个子类别），通过两层级标注协议验证（与人类标注82%一致，κ=0.73）。测试12个开源代码/推理模型共4,332个响应发现：模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成无根基代码，仅27%正确拒绝，但对可解对照组几乎不错误拒绝（0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码在现实世界中缺乏根据，可能导入不存在的包或声称实现违反已证定理的算法却仍能编译运行。现有研究未能区分"代码幻觉"（无根基生成）与普通代码错误，且缺乏系统性的分类框架和评估方法来衡量这一问题。

Method: 提出三维分类学：(1) 根基性（绝对违反普适真理vs相对捏造生态特定事实）；(2) 表现层面（语法、语义或事实）；(3) 行为（从自信捏造到退化输出），并按严重性排序组织。构建对抗性测试套件，设计故意不可满足的任务（正确回应应拒绝），配有匹配的可解对照组，采用两层级协议标注并经人类标签验证。

Result: 评估12个开源权重模型共4,332个评判响应：模型在约60%的不可满足提示上产生无根基代码，仅27%正确拒绝；对可解对照组几乎不出现错误拒绝（0%）。测试套件的标注协议与人类标签达到82%一致性（κ=0.73）。

Conclusion: 代码幻觉是LLM代码生成中的严重且普遍的问题——模型很少在任务不可满足时拒绝，而是倾向于生成看似合理但无根基的代码。该现象可通过提出的分类体系和对抗性套件进行系统化度量，表明LLM在识别生成任务的不可满足性方面存在根本缺口。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [184] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TipCoder是一种测试时指令空间探索方法，通过生成问题特定的辅助提示（tips）来补充原始代码生成指令，结合基答案与提示引导答案并通过奖励模型选择，实现指令级别的测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展主要集中于从固定指令中采样多个程序（解决方案空间探索），但许多编码失败源于原始提示本身缺失约束、忽略边界情况或包含误导性推理路径。因此需要探索互补的指令级空间来提升代码生成性能。

Method: TipCoder包含两个阶段：(1) 从多轮调试轨迹中蒸馏出主动引导（proactive guidance）来训练一个指令提议器（Proposer）；(2) 使用边际效用奖励（marginal-utility reward）通过强化学习优化该提议器。推理时同时生成基答案和提示引导答案，并用奖励模型进行事后选择。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准测试和多种目标代码大语言模型上，TipCoder均提供一致的指令级测试时扩展策略，优于在共享奖励模型选择协议下的随机采样和通用提示优化基线。

Conclusion: 这种"探索-选择"设计允许提示暴露额外的候选潜力，同时减少不必要引导带来的回归问题，为代码生成提供了一种互补于传统采样方法的指令级测试时扩展新方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [185] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对一家大型软件服务公司进行案例研究（N=21次半结构化访谈），探讨软件专业人员在组织采用AI过程中经历的心理成本，发现包括问责焦虑、工艺身份中断、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知和工作负荷强化以及不确定性困扰等五类心理成本，并揭示从业者通过恢复控制、保护性适应和容忍吸收三种方式应对这些成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织积极推动AI在软件工程中的采用，但现有AI采用策略主要关注生产力等有形成果。然而AI是颠覆性力量，会在既有的角色身份、团队规范和职业满足感的稳定环境中被引入，历史上技术颠覆曾引发焦虑、意义侵蚀、技能退化等心理和社会压力。研究者质疑"AI对软件工程无成本"的假设，希望理解软件专业人员在组织AI采用中所经历的心理成本。

Method: 在一家大型软件开发服务公司启动AI采用一年后开展案例研究，通过会议和半结构化访谈（N=21）收集定性数据，分析和归纳软件专业人员心理成本及其管理策略。

Result: 发现软件专业人员经历五类心理成本：问责焦虑（accountability anxiety）、工艺身份中断（craft identity disruption）、意义与满意度侵蚀（meaning and satisfaction erosion）、认知和工作负荷强化（cognitive and workload intensification）、不确定性困扰（uncertainty distress）。从业者通过三种方式应对：恢复控制（restore control）、保护性和身份保持性适应（protective and identity-preserving adaptations）、或容忍吸收（absorb）无法解决的负担。

Conclusion: 本研究将AI采用重新定位为一种"人的转变"（human transition），不仅是技术和组织层面的转变。研究贡献在于将AI-人类协作的讨论扩展到心理维度，强调AI在SE中的采用并非无成本，需要关注从业者的心理体验和职业身份转变。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [186] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM在代码转换规则合成方面的能力进行了实证研究，评估了三种领域特定语言（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep）下三个LLM模型在四项软件演化任务中的表现，发现强前沿模型（GPT-5.4）在规则合成上已超越概念验证阶段，但较小或开放权重模型在复杂迁移场景中仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具有黑盒特性，缺乏可解释性和确定性，且在大型代码库上的重复性任务会产生高昂使用成本。为了缓解这些问题，作者希望探索LLM能否合成可复用的、确定性的代码转换规则，从而实现更经济且可解释的代码转换方案。

Method: 针对三种领域特定转换规则语言（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep），在六个数据集上评估三个LLM（GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B、Llama3.1-8B），涵盖四项软件演化任务：API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移和语言版本迁移，并与反合一算法进行对比。

Result: GPT-5.4在大多数基准上实现了高且一致的规则适用率，生成的转换最接近真实答案。较小模型（GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B）在简单局部变更上有效，但在复杂迁移场景中表现欠佳。LLM表现出通过元变量使用的非平凡泛化能力，且多个数据集的第一个四分位数具有高复用分数。与反合一算法相比，LLM在正确性上更优，但规则适用率更低。

Conclusion: LLM在生成正确、通用且可复用的转换规则方面展现出巨大潜力，转换规则合成已超越概念验证阶段。这些规则具有确定性、可解释性，并能降低重复任务的推理成本，为缓解LLM黑盒和高成本问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [187] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文进行了一项初步研究，评估四种大型语言模型（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3）在从源代码提交中提取架构设计决策（ADDs）的能力，使用零样本和少样本提示方式测试了30个来自开源项目的ADD。结果显示所有模型BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示能改善对齐效果，但生成的ADD往往过长、偏重实现细节，且缺失决策背后的基本原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构设计决策（ADDs）蕴含软件系统结构和演化的基本原理，但很少被明确记录，常常隐藏在源代码提交中。恢复这些决策对架构知识管理（AKM）至关重要，然而从提交中提取ADDs极具挑战性。大语言模型虽有潜力，但在此任务上的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究使用四种LLM（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3），采用零样本和少样本提示两种设置，对来自开源项目的30个人工编写的ADDs进行提取测试。使用ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore四种指标评分，并由一位作者手动审查Gemini的输出结果。

Result: 所有模型在BERT-F1上均超过0.81；少样本提示改善了与参考的匹配度（如Gemini从0.828提升到0.847）。然而生成的ADDs往往过长、过于聚焦实现层面，并且遗漏了决策背后的基本原理。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在提取ADD方面有一定基础能力但仍有明显不足，尤其在对架构决策原由的捕捉上存在短板。这为构建架构感知的LLM系统和自动化AKM指明了改进方向与机遇。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [188] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"可持续性反模式"（sustainability antipatterns）的概念，用于捕捉软件工程中系统性产生不可持续结果的跨层面条件，并倡导将其作为可持续软件工程综合研究议程的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究（如能效、资源优化、代码可维护性）对可持续性问题如何在软件工程的社会-技术层面（组织、流程、产品）之间产生并相互强化缺乏解释。作者认为可持续性挑战并非孤立问题，而是系统性的——源于组织优先级、开发实践与技术条件之间的相互作用，需要新的概念框架来识别跨层面的错位。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文（vision paper）。作者引入"可持续性反模式"概念，用于捕捉系统性产生不可持续结果的条件模式；并论证该概念能帮助暴露社会-技术层面与可持续性维度之间难以通过工件中心或维度特定分析识别的跨层面错位，同时提出了识别、形式化、检测和缓解可持续性反模式的具体研究方向。

Result: 论文提出的主要成果是：确立了"可持续性反模式"作为分析框架的可行性，展示了它如何揭示组织、流程和产品层面之间被忽视的错位，并为未来研究勾勒了四条具体的行动路线（识别、形式化、检测、缓解）。

Conclusion: 作者设想可持续性反模式能够成为构建一个整合且可操作的可持续软件工程研究议程的基础，推动对软件可持续性问题进行系统性的跨层面分析与治理，而非局限于单一维度或单一层面的局部优化。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [189] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LabelMate，一个基于大语言模型的框架，能够从历史问题报告中自动推导项目特定的标签集，并在无需预标注训练数据的情况下为新问题报告自动分配相关标签。在来自30个GitHub仓库的16,500条问题报告上评估，生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达89.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件问题报告（issue reports）的标签标注对规划和社区参与很重要，但现有方法存在三大局限：需要大量人工干预、分配通用标签、依赖已有标注数据集。作者希望解决这些痛点，实现无需人工设计和预训练数据的自动化标签方案。

Method: LabelMate框架包含两大核心步骤：(1) 利用大语言模型从历史问题报告中推导出全面且项目特定的标签集；(2) 自动为新的问题报告分配相关标签，整个过程不需要任何预标注训练数据。

Result: 在30个多样化GitHub仓库的16,500条问题报告上，LabelMate生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达到89.84%，相比现有的通用标签分配方法有统计显著的改进。

Conclusion: LabelMate提供了一种高效、领域自适应的解决方案，能够简化问题标签标注流程，克服了以往方法对人工干预和预标注数据的依赖，验证了LLM驱动框架在软件问题标签领域的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [190] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在代码修复中的"过度编辑"问题，即模型重写代码超出修复bug所需范围。作者通过从400个BigCodeBench问题构建带已知最小补丁的受控评估框架，发现过度编辑在前沿模型中普遍存在（甚至高性能模型如GPT-5.5亦然）。文内提出"保留指令"可显著减少过度编辑，并比较了SFT与RL在后期训练中学习最小编辑的效果，发现RL在域外编辑保真度和性能保持上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码修复不仅需要正确性，还应具备最小性、可审查性和对原始实现的忠实度。现有研究聚焦正确性指标（如Pass@1），忽视了过度编辑问题——模型可能大规模重写代码，引入不必要的复杂度和审查负担。作者希望通过系统化度量和技术手段来解决这一被忽视的质量维度。

Method: 1) 从400个BigCodeBench问题中，通过向参考解注入受控的AST级损坏构造修复任务，每个任务都有已知的最小补丁。2) 测量前沿LLM的过度编辑程度（如多余Levenshtein距离、新增认知复杂度）。3) 测试"保留指令"（preservation instruction）对减少过度编辑的效果。4) 比较监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）在后期训练中学习最小编辑能力的表现。

Result: 1) 过度编辑在前沿模型（包括GPT-5.5）中普遍存在，高Pass@1可与不必要的大规模编辑共存。2) 保留指令将平均多余Levenshtein距离从0.195降至0.131，减少26.6%的新增认知复杂度，同时将Pass@1提高2.3个百分点。3) 这些改善并非单纯来自更大的推理预算或模型规模。4) SFT会过拟合到已见过的损坏模式，而RL在域外编辑保真度和性能保持之间取得了最佳权衡。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度（edit fidelity）应被视为代码修复质量的一个独立评价维度，它可以被有效度量（通过受控AST损坏注入）和学习（通过保留指令和RL训练策略）。该研究为代码修复模型的评估与训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [191] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATIBA是一个论文完整性检查工具，对稿件执行五项基于真实证据的检查：参考文献完整性、会议合规性、实证标准合规性、多模式AI评审和引文建议。其核心设计原则是LLM只负责判断，绝不凭空捏造证据。用户研究显示感知有用性良好（平均85%一致率），但客观准确性尚待测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文投稿前的三类检查（参考文献完整性、目标会议投稿规则合规性、社区报告标准遵循度）目前主要依靠人工，过程繁琐、重复且因会议不同而异，实际中经常执行不一致或被跳过，亟需自动化工具支持。

Method: ATIBA包含五项检查：(1)参考文献完整性检查——对照书目来源验证每条引文，标记撤稿或无法找到的文献；(2)会议/赛道合规检查——直接从会议自己的征稿页面提取投稿标准并评估稿件，每个判定锚定页面原文引用；(3)实证标准合规检查——对照ACM SIGSOFT实证标准，并带有防幻觉机制，丢弃无法在稿件中逐字定位的证据引用；(4)基于GPT-5.4（通过Azure OpenAI）的多模式AI评审；(5)引文建议功能——在展示给用户前逐条验证候选参考文献。所有检查遵循同一原则：LLM仅被信任做判断，不允许捏造其评判依据的证据。

Result: 通过包含13位非作者参与者的有主持用户研究进行评估，六个调查项目的一致率在69%至92%之间，平均85%，初步证明了所评估工作流中积极的感知有用性。

Conclusion: ATIBA建立了感知有用性的初步证据，但客观准确性仍需进一步测量验证。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [192] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Gate是一个仓库级别的软件工程基准测试，它在评估编码智能体时明确加入了来自真实PR审查评论的"审查约束"（review constraints）考量，而不只是功能测试。基准包含303个实例，覆盖75个Python仓库。实验发现644个通过功能测试的修复中有221个未能满足审查约束，说明纯功能评估高估了智能体满足完整修复需求的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准主要只评估生成的补丁是否通过功能测试，忽略了真实软件开发中来自代码审查的接受约束（review-derived acceptance constraints），这些约束常常决定补丁是否真的会被接受。因此需要一个新的基准来显式评估智能体对审查约束的遵守情况。

Method: 1) 从真实PR审查评论中提取并定义"审查约束"；2) 围绕这些约束合成仓库级修复实例；3) 每个实例分别提供功能测试和约束测试，以及不合规补丁与黄金补丁；4) 构建303个实例，覆盖75个开源Python仓库；5) 使用四个不同能力级别的LLM后端在统一编码智能体脚手架下进行实验评估。

Result: 在644个通过功能测试的修复中，有221个未能满足提供的审查约束，表明功能成功与完整修复规范成功之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 仅基于功能的评估会高估智能体在仓库级修复任务中满足完整需求的能力，评估基准应将审查约束合规性纳入考量以更真实地反映实际软件开发中的接受标准。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering benchmarks have significantly advanced the evaluation of coding agents, but existing benchmarks primarily measure whether generated patches pass functional tests and overlook review-derived acceptance constraints (review constraints) that often influence whether a patch is acceptable in real-world software development. We introduce SWE-Gate, a repository-level benchmark for software engineering agents that explicitly evaluates review constraint compliance alongside functional correctness. SWE-Gate derives review constraints from real pull request review comments and synthesizes repository-level repair instances around these constraints. Each instance provides separate functional and constraint tests, together with non-compliant and gold patches, enabling explicit separation between issue resolution capability and review constraint compliance. We construct SWE-Gate with 303 repository-level repair instances spanning 75 open-source Python repositories across diverse software domains. Experiments with four LLM backends spanning different capability levels under a common coding-agent scaffold reveal a substantial gap between functional success and success under the complete repair specification: among 644 repairs that pass the functional tests, 221 fail to satisfy the provided review constraints. These findings show that functional-only evaluation overestimates agents' ability to satisfy the full requirements of repository-level repair tasks. The replication package including code, data, and experimental results is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/SWE-Gate.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个"无知方向"（direction of ignorance），它编码了训练语料中的unigram分布，作为模型在不确定时的贝叶斯先验。通过将最终预测状态投影到该方向，可得到逐token的先验载荷因子λ，该因子随上下文信息量增加而下降。投影将预测状态分解为正交向量，恰好对应带温度参数（tempered）的贝叶斯更新中的两项：unigram先验（指数为λ）和上下文驱动的似然。该几何-概率解释使λ可跨模型大小和家族比较，更大模型在高上下文场景下先验依赖更低。最后通过因果干预验证：提高或降低λ可在KL散度上引导预测靠近或远离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息不足时的预测机制：模型缺乏线索时如何回退到训练语料的统计先验，以及这种回退在几何结构和概率意义上如何实现。

Method: - 识别unembedding矩阵中编码unigram分布的单方向（direction of ignorance），在四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，0.4B-405B参数）中验证其存在。 - 将最终预测状态投影到此方向，定义先验载荷因子λ，并证明该投影可分解预测状态为两个正交向量，与带有温度参数的贝叶斯更新（unigram先验^λ × 上下文似然）精确对应。 - 通过因果干预实验验证方向的有效性：调节λ可引导预测靠近或远离unigram先验。

Result: - "无知方向"在所有四个模型家族中普遍存在。 - λ随上下文信息量增加稳定下降。 - 该几何结构精确对应tempered贝叶斯更新的因式分解。 - λ可跨模型大小和家族进行有意义的比较，大模型在高上下文下的先验依赖更低。 - 该方向因果活跃，干预λ可有效调控预测与先验之间的距离（KL散度）。

Conclusion: 语言模型在不确定性下的回退行为具有清晰的几何与概率双重解释：unembedding矩阵中的"无知方向"编码了unigram先验，预测状态在该方向上的投影系数λ量化了先验的加权程度，且该结构在大规模模型上具有因果影响力。这项工作桥接了神经网络的几何表示与贝叶斯推理框架，为理解语言模型的不确定性处理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [194] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"（equation recast）方法，将参数化算子学习重新表述为学习单一标准算子。参数引起的算子变化通过控制方程解析推导并吸收到有效源项中，从而在参数回归预测中实现零样本外推。该方法在多变参数、非线性和奇异PDE设置中验证了其外推能力、异构数据整合能力以及利用收敛性丢失作为内部失败警告信号的特性。在核聚变高保真托卡马克模拟中，该框架通过标准域映射统一了四个装置几何的电子温度数据于单一联合训练算子中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化模型需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数的数据，且可能在没有警告的情况下在训练分布之外失败。这促使研究者寻找一种能跨越广泛参数范围、具备外推能力且可监控推理的方法。

Method: 将参数化算子学习重铸为单一标准算子学习。通过解析地从控制方程推导参数引起的算子变化，并将这些变化吸收为"有效源项"，从而建立不同参数范围间的标准映射（canonical mapping）。

Result: 在多变参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中支持外推；能以共享标准表示整合稀疏异构数据集；通过重铸迭代的收敛性丢失作为模型失效的内部警告信号。在托卡马克核聚变高保真模拟中，成功将四个装置几何的电子温度数据统一到一个联合训练算子中。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了路径，结合了方程引导的迁移、数据效率和可监控的推理，有望替代传统纯数据驱动的参数化算子学习方法。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [195] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习研究进展，涵盖其基本原理、分类体系、问题形式化及开放挑战，旨在整合这一新兴领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式机器学习在可扩展性和隐私方面存在根本性局限，而协作学习（联邦学习、去中心化学习）虽已引起关注，但大多研究聚焦于欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），对图结构数据在协作环境下的学习研究仍严重不足。

Method: 从三个核心维度（学习有效性、学习效率、隐私保护）综述欧几里得数据的协作学习基本原理；提出图分布场景分类体系，刻画统计异构性，并建立标准化问题形式化和算法框架。

Result: 构建了从欧几里得到图结构数据的协作学习综合综述框架，提出了图分布场景的taxonomy分类，并系统识别了开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 图上的消息传递机制天然适合协作环境中的信息交换，但该领域仍处于早期阶段，存在诸多待探索的研究方向。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [196] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归二次滤波器（RQFs），这是一种受生物启发的复值时域滤波器，属于对角状态空间模型（SSMs）的特例。作者研究如何通过使每层的自底向上输入"前瞻化"（prospective）来缓解深层连续时间循环网络中的时序传感延迟和梯度衰减问题。该方法在所有模型和配置下匹配或超越非前瞻对照，在Speech Commands和Path-X任务上取得出色结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间循环网络通过时间整合获得记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中，时间整合会延迟自底向上的信号并衰减自顶向下的误差，这是一个需要解决的失败模式。

Method: 1) 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。2) 使用参数自由的二抽头更新（two-tap update）使每层自底向上输入前瞻化，保持循环转置和并行扫描不变。3) 将该校正推广到一般对角SSMs，证明其在时间梯度截断（仅空间反向传播）时缓解深度依赖的梯度衰减。

Result: 在RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs（非线性门控RNN）上评估前瞻干预。全BPTT下前瞻变体在所有模型和配置中匹配或超越非前瞻对照。非残差宽度32六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率；宽度64六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达到83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的循环基底，前瞻输入编码是对深层连续时间循环网络的一种输入侧校正方案，能有效解决深度堆叠中的时序延迟和梯度衰减问题。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [197] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于头级功能组织的实证基础来指导混合注意力架构设计。通过两个干预指标（RFIS和RPD），在RoPE Transformer中发现了检索头和位置头的完整分类学，并定义全局位置带（GPBand）。基于此提出两个设计原则，并实例化为头级混合架构（HwH），使用NoPE FA进行全局检索、LA进行局部位置建模，在低FA比例下仍保持强性能并显著提升零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全注意力（FA）和线性注意力（LA）混合架构的分配方式仍是启发式的，缺乏证据支撑。作者希望从RoPE Transformer学习到的头级功能组织中寻找实证依据，以指导更合理的混合架构设计。

Method: 提出两个干预指标：RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数）衡量每个频率对注意力分布的影响，RPD（RoPE位置依赖性）隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖。在Qwen3和Llama3.1上验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学。进一步提出两个设计原则：位置建模仅局部操作，全局访问通过位置无关检索实现；两种功能应在头粒度上分配并逐层配置。据此构建HwH架构，用NoPE FA做全局检索，LA做局部位置建模。

Result: HwH在FA与LA比例低于1:3的情况下，仍保持强语言建模和常识推理能力，同时改进检索能力并显著增强零样本长上下文外推，优于Transformer、LA和逐层混合基线。消融实验验证了两个原则和各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了零样本长度外推失败的潜在原因（全局位置带边界与训练长度相关），并证明基于原理的混合架构设计是构建未来基础模型的有前景路径。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [198] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观与折扣），用于任意N人标准形式博弈，每位玩家至多K个动作。该算法保证O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾，并统一在博弈过程中的时间跨度上成立。该算法将折扣(N+1)阶预测器与熵正则化结合，在一个游戏中策略空间的适当"提升"上运行乐观FTRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前尝试在一般博弈中实现恒定遗憾（常数遗憾）的算法都受阻于诱导博弈序列出现大幅波动这一关键障碍。作者希望通过设计一个能够以受控方式阻尼这种大振幅振荡的算法，从而突破这一瓶颈，在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾界。

Method: 提出更高阶乐观与折扣（HOOD）算法，其核心是乐观FTRL（OptFTRL）的一个变体，包含两个关键组分：(1) 折扣的(N+1)阶预测器，用于更精细地预测对手行为；(2) 在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"上的熵正则化。两者的组合旨在以受控方式阻尼诱导的游戏序列的大规模振荡。

Result: 对任意N人且每人至多K个动作的一般标准形式博弈，HOOD算法保证O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾界，该界在整个博弈时间跨度上均匀成立，显著优于此前的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作与Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar的独立工作（获得O(N²¹log⁴K)遗憾界）具有显著相似性，进一步验证了高阶乐观方法在一般博弈中获得常数遗憾的可行性。HOOD在参数依赖上明显更优（N³ vs N²¹）。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [199] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，该方法不再仅优化平均奖励，而是直接优化策略在奖励上尾（高奖励区域）的概率覆盖。TailRL通过最大化超过随机选取奖励阈值的对数概率，使梯度更关注罕见的高奖励样本，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。它只需简单修改优势函数，即可兼容现有RL流程，并在多个任务中证明能利用罕见高奖励训练样本避免次优解，提升推理时额外采样的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习通常优化平均奖励，但对于生成式策略，平均奖励掩盖了一个重要区别：两个策略可能有相同的平均奖励，但产生罕见高奖励rollout的概率差异很大。随着训练和推理时采样增加，其收益取决于在高奖励结果上保留概率质量的能力，因此需要直接优化这种覆盖率。

Method: TailRL将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件（对每个奖励阈值，考虑策略超过该阈值的概率），通过最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率来优化覆盖。其梯度给予罕见高奖励rollout更多权重，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。实现上只需对优势函数进行简单修改，即可兼容现有RL管线。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务上，TailRL能够利用罕见的、高奖励的训练样本避免陷入次优解，并使模型在推理时从额外采样中获益更多。

Conclusion: TailRL通过关注奖励分布的尾部而非仅平均值，提供了一种更有效的策略优化方式，尤其适用于高奖励rollout稀疏的场景，且实现轻量、兼容性强。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [200] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为具有转移不确定性的通用和并发随机博弈（CSGs）提出了PAC学习框架，同时处理纳什均衡存在性的挑战。算法通过数据驱动的L1置信集和鲁棒CSG求解，要么返回近似最优的ε-近似的社会福利最优纳什均衡，要么提供不存在精确NE的可靠认证，且样本复杂度为多项式级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通用和并发随机博弈中，学习纳什均衡面临两大核心挑战：一是转移核的不确定性使得均衡求解困难，二是纳什均衡在一般和博弈中可能不存在，导致现有的PAC学习框架无法直接适用。因此需要一个统一的框架，既能有效学习近似均衡，又能对均衡是否存在给出有原则的判断。

Method: 算法维护数据驱动的L1置信集来刻画转移核的不确定性，并求解鲁棒CSG以获得社会福利最优的ε-NE。采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖。关键创新是引入纳什边际（Nash margin）刻画，使框架能够对均衡存在性进行原理性推理：要么返回ε-近似NE（且社会福利值ε-接近最优），要么给出不存在精确NE的可靠证书。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max² H⁴ |S|² |A| / (p_reach ε²))。在基准CSG上的实验表明：近似最优性能、正确处理均衡存在与不存在的情况、样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了通用和并发随机博弈中PAC学习理论的空白，首次将均衡存在性推理纳入学习框架，为在不确定环境下的多智能体博弈学习提供了坚实的理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


### [201] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散方程的基本未知量（静电势、电子和空穴准费米能级），通过数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差联合训练嵌入物理，具备尺寸泛化能力和主动学习机制，在保证高保真的同时将吞吐量提升数个数量级，实现跨器件尺度的Pareto前沿探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散模拟虽精确但计算成本高昂，尤其3D结构中随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重限制多目标设计空间探索。现有ML替代模型仅将固定设计参数映射到少量标量指标，丢弃底层物理，缺乏跨几何和器件族的可迁移性。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络(GAT)替代模型：直接在四面体TCAD网格上操作，将网格建模为图，在每个节点预测静电势、电子和空穴准费米能级三个基本未知量；训练目标结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差；采用深度集成提供逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环筛选高信息量设计进行全模拟。

Result: 在multi-fin三栅FinFET上以Sentaurus Device为基准，模型以每场亚伏级RMSE复现三个漂移-扩散场，单设计吞吐量比全模拟器高数个数量级；优势随器件尺寸增长，大型multi-fin阵列推断仍可在每器件亚秒内完成，误差源于尺寸泛化（少fin训练的模型可直接应用于大阵列）。

Conclusion: 该物理信息GAT替代模型突破了传统ML代理的可迁移性瓶颈，继承了网格拓扑的尺寸泛化能力，使跨器件尺度的Pareto前沿探索成为可能，而这些尺度对于直接TCAD扫描是不可行的。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [202] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种图网络模拟器，通过将交互历史直接存储在接触边上（而非节点特征或节点级记忆），配合注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久化边记忆，并通过边身份字典在接触图动态变化时保留记忆。物理结构化解码器预测颗粒间法向和切向接触力，强制库仑摩擦极限。在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，TRACE相比GNS和NMGNS将长滚转位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时参数更少且颗粒互穿近乎为零，并相比MPM参考求解器实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒动力学强烈依赖颗粒间接触历史，但现有图模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，难以在颗粒接触形成、断裂和重新排列时有效保留接触历史，这限制了长时程模拟的物理一致性和精度。

Method: TRACE在每条接触边上维护持久化记忆，利用基于注意力的消息传递和门控循环单元（GRU）更新边缘记忆；采用边身份字典在动态接触图中保留边缘记忆；解码器基于物理结构预测法向和切向接触力，强制库仑摩擦极限并施加等大反向内力；训练采用单步预训练加自回归展开微调的两阶段策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE产生稳定且物理一致的长时程滚转，精确复现最终沉积几何和坍塌中释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，长滚转位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，参数更少且互穿近乎为零。相比MPM求解器实现12.2倍（2D）和8.9倍（3D）加速。

Conclusion: 将交互历史存储在接触边上并配合记忆保持机制，是提升颗粒图模拟器长时程精度和物理一致性的有效途径。TRACE为颗粒动力学的可学习模拟提供了更高效、更精确的替代方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [203] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLIWBO（有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化），从有限平滑输入映射库中选择扭曲函数来适应输入几何结构，在保留高概率收敛保证的同时提升GP-BO的样本效率。通过理论分析证明了有限库大小带来的平方根代价，并在多个基准上验证了其优于原始坐标GP-UCB及手工缩放的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO的收敛保证（如GP-UCB）要求固定核函数，但核函数编码的输入几何与原始坐标可能不匹配（如对数尺度超参数或局部峰值），导致样本效率低下。输入扭曲可改善此问题，但已知GP-UCB证明无法在变化核下成立，缺乏既能自适应输入几何又保留收敛保证的方法。

Method: FLIWBO从有限平滑输入映射库中选取扭曲映射，允许任意历史依赖的选择规则，融合输入几何适应与高概率收敛保证。理论分析在温和假设下给出收敛界，并显式展示了库大小N_ε的平方根代价。

Result: 受控诊断表明有限库扭曲能修复几何不匹配并揭示失败情况。在四个重复基准（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST HPO）中，FLIWBO-UCB优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能逃脱击败预知扭曲EI的陷阱，并恢复手动对数缩放的大部分收益，在具备匹配遗憾保证的方法中领先。20维MAS设计研究验证了其在高代价噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO成功弥合了自适应输入几何与可证明收敛保证之间的鸿沟，提供了一种实用且理论上自洽的GP-BO增强方案，代码已开源供复现。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases.
